progress_interval_sec INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 120;`,\n273\t\n274\t\t\t\t// Version 16 — add workers table for spawn_worker synthetic tool and max_workers to groups.\n275\t\t\t\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN max_workers INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5;\n276\t\n277\t\t\t\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workers (\n278\t\t\t\t\tid           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n279\t\t\t\t\tchat_id      INTEGER NOT NULL,\n280\t\t\t\t\tthread_id    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n281\t\t\t\t\tparent_msg   INTEGER NOT NULL,\n282\t\t\t\t\tprompt       TEXT NOT NULL,\n283\t\t\t\t\tsession_id   TEXT,\n284\t\t\t\t\tmodel        TEXT,\n285\t\t\t\t\tstatus       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running',\n286\t\t\t\t\tresult       TEXT,\n287\t\t\t\t\terror        TEXT,\n288\t\t\t\t\tstarted_at   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n289\t\t\t\t\tfinished_at  TEXT\n290\t\t\t\t);\n291\t\n292\t\t\t\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_chat_thread ON workers(chat_id, thread_id);\n293\t\t\t\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_status ON workers(status);`,\n294\t\n295\t\t\t\t// Version 17 — add dispatcher_mode to groups and sessions for orchestrator system prompt injection.\n296\t\t\t\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN dispatcher_mode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;\n297\t\t\t\t ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN dispatcher_mode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT -1;`,\n298\t\n299\t\t\t\t// Version 18 — add from_user_id to cost_events for per-user attribution.\n300\t\t\t\t`ALTER TABLE cost_events ADD COLUMN from_user_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`,\n301\t\t}\n302\t// OpenDB opens (or creates) the SQLite database at path, enables WAL mode,\n303\t// and applies any pending migrations.\n304\tfunc OpenDB(path string) (*DB, error) {\n305\t\tdb, err := sql.Open(\"sqlite\", path)\n306\t\tif err != nil {\n307\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open sqlite %q: %w\", path, err)\n308\t\t}\n309\t\n310\t\t// SQLite works best with a single writer connection.\n311\t\tdb.SetMaxOpenConns(1)\n312\t\n313\t\tif _, err := db.Exec(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;\"); err != nil {\n314\t\t\tdb.Close()\n315\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enable WAL: %w\", err)\n316\t\t}\n317\t\tif _, err := db.Exec(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;\"); err != nil {\n318\t\t\tdb.Close()\n319\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enable foreign keys: %w\", err)\n320\t\t}\n321\t\n322\t\td := &DB{db: db}\n323\t\tif err := d.migrate(); err != nil {\n324\t\t\tdb.Close()\n325\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"migrate: %w\", err)\n326\t\t}\n327\t\treturn d, nil\n328\t}\n329\t\n330\t// Close closes the underlying database connection.\n331\tfunc (d *DB) Close() error {\n332\t\treturn d.db.Close()\n333\t}\n334\t\n335\t// SqlDB returns the underlying *sql.DB for use with other packages.\n336\tfunc (d *DB) SqlDB() *sql.DB {\n337\t\treturn d.db\n338\t}\n339\t\n340\t// migrate creates the schema_version table if needed, then applies any\n341\t// migrations whose version number exceeds the current schema version.\n342\tfunc (d *DB) migrate() error {\n343\t\t_, err := d.db.Exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_version (\n344\t\t\tversion    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n345\t\t\tapplied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n346\t\t);`)\n347\t\tif err != nil {\n348\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create schema_version: %w\", err)\n349\t\t}\n350\t\n351\t\tvar current int\n352\t\trow := d.db.QueryRow(`SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) FROM schema_version`)\n353\t\tif err := row.Scan(&current); err != nil {\n354\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"read schema_version: %w\", err)\n355\t\t}\n356\t\n357\t\tfor i, ddl := range migrations {\n358\t\t\tversion := i + 1\n359\t\t\tif version <= current {\n360\t\t\t\tcontinue\n361\t\t\t}\n362\t\n363\t\t\ttx, err := d.db.Begin()\n364\t\t\tif err != nil {\n365\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"begin migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n366\t\t\t}\n367\t\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(ddl); err != nil {\n368\t\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n369\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"apply migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n370\t\t\t}\n371\t\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(\n372\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO schema_version (version) VALUES (?)`, version,\n373\t\t\t); err != nil {\n374\t\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n375\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n376\t\t\t}\n377\t\t\tif err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {\n378\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"commit migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n379\t\t\t}\n380\t\t}\n381\t\treturn nil\n382\t}\n383\t\n384\t// ── groups ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n385\t\n386\t// GetGroup returns the group with the given chat_id, or (nil, nil) if not found.\n387\tfunc (d *DB) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) (*Group, error) {\n388\t\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n389\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n390\t\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n391\t\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n392\t\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n393\t\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n394\t\t\t FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID)\n395\t\treturn scanGroup(row)\n396\t}\n397\t\n398\t// UpsertGroup inserts or replaces a group record.\n399\tfunc (d *DB) UpsertGroup(ctx context.Context, g *Group) error {\n400\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n401\t\t\t`INSERT INTO groups (chat_id, name, cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec, permission_mode, allowed_tools, disallowed_tools, max_subtasks, max_workers, progress_interval_sec, dispatcher_mode, created_at)\n402\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n403\t\t\t ON CONFLICT(chat_id) DO UPDATE SET\n404\t\t\t   name                = excluded.name,\n405\t\t\t   cwd                  = excluded.cwd,\n406\t\t\t   default_model       = excluded.default_model,\n407\t\t\t   max_budget          = excluded.max_budget,\n408\t\t\t   timeout_sec         = excluded.timeout_sec,\n409\t\t\t   permission_mode      = excluded.permission_mode,\n410\t\t\t   allowed_tools        = excluded.allowed_tools,\n411\t\t\t   disallowed_tools     = excluded.disallowed_tools,\n412\t\t\t   max_subtasks         = excluded.max_subtasks,\n413\t\t\t   max_workers          = excluded.max_workers,\n414\t\t\t   progress_interval_sec = excluded.progress_interval_sec,\n415\t\t\t   dispatcher_mode      = excluded.dispatcher_mode`,\n416\t\t\tg.ChatID, g.Name, g.CWD, g.DefaultModel, g.MaxBudget, g.TimeoutSec, g.PermissionMode,\n417\t\t\tnullableString(g.AllowedTools), nullableString(g.DisallowedTools),\n418\t\t\tg.MaxSubtasks, g.MaxWorkers, g.ProgressIntervalSec, g.DispatcherMode,\n419\t\t\tg.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n420\t\t)\n421\t\treturn err\n422\t}\n423\t\n424\t// ListGroups returns all configured groups.\n425\tfunc (d *DB) ListGroups(ctx context.Context) ([]*Group, error) {\n426\t\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n427\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n428\t\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n429\t\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n430\t\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n431\t\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n432\t\t\t FROM groups ORDER BY created_at`)\n433\t\tif err != nil {\n434\t\t\treturn nil, err\n435\t\t}\n436\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n437\t\n438\t\tvar groups []*Group\n439\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n440\t\t\tg, err := scanGroup(rows)\n441\t\t\tif err != nil {\n442\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n443\t\t\t}\n444\t\t\tgroups = append(groups, g)\n445\t\t}\n446\t\treturn groups, rows.Err()\n447\t}\n448\t\n449\t// DeleteGroup removes a group and all its sessions (cascade handled manually).\n450\tfunc (d *DB) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) error {\n451\t\ttx, err := d.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)\n452\t\tif err != nil {\n453\t\t\treturn err\n454\t\t}\n455\t\tdefer tx.Rollback()\n456\t\n457\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n458\t\t\treturn err\n459\t\t}\n460\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n461\t\t\treturn err\n462\t\t}\n463\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n464\t\t\treturn err\n465\t\t}\n466\t\treturn tx.Commit()\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\ttype groupScanner interface {\n470\t\tScan(dest ...any) error\n471\t}\n472\t\n473\tfunc scanGroup(s groupScanner) (*Group, error) {\n474\t\tvar g Group\n475\t\tvar createdAt string\n476\t\terr := s.Scan(&g.ChatID, &g.Name, &g.CWD, &g.DefaultModel, &g.MaxBudget, &g.TimeoutSec, &g.PermissionMode, &g.AllowedTools, &g.DisallowedTools, &g.MaxSubtasks, &g.MaxWorkers, &g.ProgressIntervalSec, &g.DispatcherMode, &createdAt)\n477\t\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n478\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n479\t\t}\n480\t\tif err != nil {\n481\t\t\treturn nil, err\n482\t\t}\n483\t\tg.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n484\t\treturn &g, nil\n485\t}\n486\t\n487\t// ── sessions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n488\t\n489\t// GetSession returns the session for (chatID, threadID), or (nil, nil) if not found.\n490\tfunc (d *DB) GetSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (*Session, error) {\n491\t\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n492\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n493\t\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n494\t\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n495\t\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n496\t\treturn scanSession(row)\n497\t}\n498\t\n499\t// CreateSession inserts a new session record.\n500\tfunc (d *DB) CreateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n501\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n502\t\tif s.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n503\t\t\ts.CreatedAt = now\n504\t\t}\n505\t\tif s.LastActive.IsZero() {\n506\t\t\ts.LastActive = now\n507\t\t}\n508\t\tif s.Status == \"\" {\n509\t\t\ts.Status = \"active\"\n510\t\t}\n511\t\tif s.IconColor == 0 {\n512\t\t\ts.IconColor = 7322096 // Default to light blue (0x6FB9F0)\n513\t\t}\n514\t\tif s.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n515\t\t\ts.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n516\t\t}\n517\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n518\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sessions\n519\t\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, model, status, icon_color, created_at, last_active, message_count, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd, summary, notification_mode, timeout_sec)\n520\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n521\t\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID, s.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n522\t\t\ts.IconColor,\n523\t\t\ts.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n524\t\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n525\t\t\ts.MessageCount,\n526\t\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n527\t\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n528\t\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n529\t\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n530\t\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n531\t\t)\n532\t\treturn err\n533\t}\n534\t\n535\t// UpdateSession updates mutable fields on an existing session.\n536\tfunc (d *DB) UpdateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n537\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n538\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n539\t\t\t SET session_id    = ?,\n540\t\t\t     cwd           = ?,\n541\t\t\t     model         = ?,\n542\t\t\t     status        = ?,\n543\t\t\t     icon_color    = ?,\n544\t\t\t     last_active   = ?,\n545\t\t\t     message_count = ?,\n546\t\t\t     pinned_message_id = ?,\n547\t\t\t     total_cost_usd    = ?,\n548\t\t\t     summary           = ?,\n549\t\t\t     notification_mode = ?,\n550\t\t\t     timeout_sec       = ?,\n551\t\t\t     dispatcher_mode   = ?\n552\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n553\t\t\ts.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n554\t\t\ts.IconColor,\n555\t\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n556\t\t\ts.MessageCount,\n557\t\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n558\t\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n559\t\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n560\t\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n561\t\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n562\t\t\ts.DispatcherMode,\n563\t\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID,\n564\t\t)\n565\t\treturn err\n566\t}\n567\t\n568\t// SetSessionIconColor updates only the icon_color field for a session.\n569\tfunc (d *DB) SetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, color int) error {\n570\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n571\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET icon_color = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n572\t\t\tcolor, chatID, threadID,\n573\t\t)\n574\t\treturn err\n575\t}\n576\t\n577\t// SetSessionNotificationMode updates only the notification_mode field for a session.\n578\t// Valid modes are: \"live\" (default), \"summary\", \"quiet\".\n579\tfunc (d *DB) SetSessionNotificationMode(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, mode string) error {\n580\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n581\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET notification_mode = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n592\t\t\tmode, chatID, threadID,\n593\t\t)\n594\t\treturn err\n595\t}\n596\t\n597\t// GetSessionIconColor returns the current icon_color for a session.\n598\t// Returns the default active color if session not found.\n599\tfunc (d *DB) GetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) int {\n600\t\tsession, err := d.GetSession(ctx, chatID, threadID)\n601\t\tif err != nil || session == nil {\n602\t\t\treturn ColorActive\n603\t\t}\n604\t\treturn session.IconColor\n605\t}\n606\t\n607\t// TouchSession bumps last_active and increments message_count atomically.\n608\tfunc (d *DB) TouchSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n609\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n610\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n611\t\t\t SET last_active   = datetime('now'),\n612\t\t\t     message_count = message_count + 1\n613\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? ORDER BY last_active DESC`, chatID)\n626\t\tif err != nil {\n627\t\t\treturn nil, err\n628\t\t}\n629\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n630\t\n631\t\tvar sessions []*Session\n632\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n633\t\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n634\t\t\tif err != nil {\n635\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n636\t\t\t}\n637\t\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n638\t\t}\n639\t\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n640\t}\n641\t\n642\t// ListAllSessions returns all sessions across all groups, ordered by last_active descending.\n643\tfunc (d *DB) ListAllSessions(ctx context.Context) ([]*Session, error) {\n644\t\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n645\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n646\t\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n647\t\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n648\t\t\t FROM sessions ORDER BY last_active DESC`)\n649\t\tif err != nil {\n650\t\t\treturn nil, err\n651\t\t}\n652\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n653\t\n654\t\tvar sessions []*Session\n655\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n656\t\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n657\t\t\tif err != nil {\n658\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n659\t\t\t}\n660\t\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n661\t\t}\n662\t\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n663\t}\n664\t\n665\t// CloseSession marks a session as closed.\n666\tfunc (d *DB) CloseSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n667\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n668\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET status = 'closed' WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n722\t\t\tstatus, chatID, threadID,\n723\t\t)\n724\t\treturn err\n725\t}\n726\t\n727\t// UpdateSessionCost adds to the total_cost_usd for a session.\n728\tfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, costUSD float64) error {\n729\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n730\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET total_cost_usd = total_cost_usd + ? WHERE chat_id = ? error\n986\t}\n987\t\n988\tfunc scanSession(s sessionScanner) (*Session, error) {\n989\t\tvar sess Session\n990\t\tvar createdAt, lastActive string\n991\t\terr := s.Scan(\n992\t\t\t&sess.ChatID, &sess.ThreadID, &sess.SessionID, &sess.CWD,\n993\t\t\t&sess.Model, &sess.Status, &createdAt, &lastActive, &sess.MessageCount, &sess.IconColor,\n994\t\t\t&sess.PinnedMessageID, &sess.TotalCostUSD, &sess.Summary, &sess.NotificationMode, &sess.TimeoutSec,\n995\t\t\t&sess.DispatcherMode,\n996\t\t)\n997\t\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n998\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n999\t\t}\n1000\t\tif err != nil {\n1001\t\t\treturn nil, err\n1002\t\t}\n1003\t\tsess.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n1004\t\tsess.LastActive, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, lastActive)\n1005\t\tif sess.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n1006\t\t\tsess.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n1007\t\t}\n1008\t\treturn &sess, nil\n1009\t}\n1010\t\n1011\t// ── allowed_users ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1012\t\n1013\t// IsUserAllowed returns true if userID is in the allowed_users table.\n1014\tfunc (d *DB) IsUserAllowed(ctx {\n1065\t\t\tu.AddedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n1066\t\t}\n1067\t\tif u.Role == \"\" {\n1068\t\t\tu.Role = \"user\"\n1069\t\t}\n1070\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n1071\t\t\t`INSERT INTO allowed_users the background_jobs.go file has the in-memory version with Cmd.\ntype BackgroundJob struct {\n\tID        string    // Unique job identifier (8-character hex)\n\tChatID    int64     // Telegram chat ID\n\tThreadID  int64     // Telegram thread ID\n\tCommand   string    // The full command being executed\n\tStatus    string    // \"running\", \"complete\", \"error\", \"interrupted\"\n\tExitCode  *int      // Exit code (nil if still running or interrupted)\n\tStartedAt time.Time // When the job was started\n}\n\nconst schemaVersion = 18\n\n// migrations is an ordered list of SQL statements applied once on startup.\n// Each entry is applied inside a single transaction. Migrations are idempotent\n// because schema_version prevents re-application.\nvar migrations = []string{\n\t// Version 1 — initial schema\n\t`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS groups (\n\t\tchat_id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n\t\tname          TEXT,\n\t\tcwd           TEXT NOT NULL,\n\t\tdefault_model TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'claude-sonnet-4-6',\n\t\tmax_budget    REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 5.0,\n\t\ttimeout_sec   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 300,\n\t\tcreated_at    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n\t);\n\n\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (\n\t\tchat_id       INTEGER NOT (chat_id, thread_id),\n\t\tFOREIGN KEY (chat_id) REFERENCES groups(chat_id)\n\t);\n\n\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS allowed_users (\n\t\tuser_id  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n\t\trole     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',\n\t\tadded_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n\t);\n\n\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sent_messages (\n\t\tchat_id    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\tthread_id  INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\tmessage_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\tpurpose    TEXT NOT NULL,\n\t\tcreated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n\t\tPRIMARY KEY (chat_id, thread_id, message_id)\n\t);`,\n\n\t// Version 2 — add permission_mode to groups\n\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN permission_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'acceptEdits';`,\n\n\t// Version 3 — add icon_color to sessions\n\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN icon_color INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 7322096;`, // 0x6FB9F0 (light blue)\n\n\t\t// Version 4 — add pinned_message_id to sessions\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN pinned_message_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 5 — add total_cost_usd to sessions\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN total_cost_usd REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 6 — add summary to sessions\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN summary TEXT;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 7 — add cost_events table for detailed cost tracking\n\t\t`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cost_events (\n\t\t\tid          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\t\t\tchat_id     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\t\tthread_id   INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\t\tcost_usd    REAL NOT NULL,\n\t\t\tinput_tokens  INTEGER,\n\t\t\toutput_tokens INTEGER,\n\t\t\tcache_read_tokens INTEGER,\n\t\t\tcache_creation_tokens INTEGER,\n\t\t\tmodel       TEXT,\n\t\t\tcreated_at  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n\t\t);\n\n\t\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cost_events_chat_thread ON cost_events(chat_id, thread_id);\n\t\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cost_events_created_at ON cost_events(created_at);`,\n\t\t// Version 8 — add notification_mode to sessions\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN notification_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'live';`,\n\n\t\t// Version 9 — add tool restrictions to groups\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN allowed_tools TEXT;\n\t\t ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN disallowed_tools TEXT;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 10 — raise default timeout from 300s to 1800s for existing groups.\n\t\t// Only updates groups still at the old hardcoded default (300); groups\n\t\t// explicitly configured to another value are left untouched.\n\t\t// timeout_sec = 0 is now the sentinel for \"no timeout\".\n\t\t`UPDATE groups SET timeout_sec = 1800 WHERE timeout_sec = 300;`,\n\t\t// Version 11 — add timeout_sec to sessions for per-topic timeout override.\n\t\t// Default 0 means \"use group timeout\" (group-level fallback).\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN timeout_sec INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 12 — add max_subtasks to groups for parallel subtask limiting.\n\t\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN max_subtasks INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5;`,\n\n\t\t// Version 13 — add subtasks table for parallel task orchestration.\n\t\t`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subtasks (\n\t\t\tid           TEXT TEXT,\n\t\t\tstatus       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running',\n\t\t\tresult       TEXT,\n\t\t\terror        TEXT,\n\t\t\tstarted_at   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n\t\t\tfinished_at 'running',\n\t\t\t\tresult       TEXT,\n\t\t\t\terror        TEXT,\n\t\t\t\tstarted_at   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n\t\t\t\tfinished_at  TEXT\n\t\t\t);\n\n\t\t\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT sessions for orchestrator system prompt injection.\n\t\t\t`ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN dispatcher_mode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;\n\t\t\t ALTER TABLE sessions ADD COLUMN dispatcher_mode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT -1;`,\n\n\t\t\t// Version 18 — add from_user_id to cost_events for per-user attribution.\n\t\t\t`ALTER TABLE cost_events ADD COLUMN from_user_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`,\n\t}\n// OpenDB opens (or creates) the SQLite database at path, enables WAL mode,\n// and applies any pending migrations.\nfunc OpenDB(path string) (*DB, error) {\n\tdb, err := sql.Open(\"sqlite\", path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open sqlite %q: %w\", path, err)\n\t}\n\n\t// SQLite works best with a single writer connection.\n\tdb.SetMaxOpenConns(1)\n\n\tif _, err := db.Exec(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;\"); err != nil {\n\t\tdb.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enable WAL: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := db.Exec(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;\"); err != nil {\n\t\tdb.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enable foreign keys: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\td := &DB{db: db}\n\tif err := d.migrate(); err != nil {\n\t\tdb.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"migrate: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn d, nil\n}\n\n// Close closes the underlying database connection.\nfunc (d *DB) Close() error {\n\treturn d.db.Close()\n}\n\n// SqlDB returns the underlying *sql.DB for use with other packages.\nfunc (d *DB) SqlDB() *sql.DB {\n\treturn d.db\n}\n\n// migrate creates the schema_version table if needed, then applies any\n// migrations whose version number exceeds the current schema version.\nfunc (d *DB) migrate() error {\n\t_, err := d.db.Exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_version (\n\t\tversion    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\tapplied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n\t);`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create schema_version: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar current int\n\trow := d.db.QueryRow(`SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) FROM schema_version`)\n\tif err := row.Scan(&current); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"read schema_version: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfor i, ddl := range migrations {\n\t\tversion := i + 1\n\t\tif version <= current {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttx, err := d.db.Begin()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"begin migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(ddl); err != nil {\n\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"apply migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO schema_version (version) VALUES (?)`, version,\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"commit migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ── groups ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// GetGroup returns the group with the given chat_id, or (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) (*Group, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n\t\t FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID)\n\treturn scanGroup(row)\n}\n\n// UpsertGroup inserts or replaces a group record.\nfunc (d *DB) UpsertGroup(ctx context.Context, g *Group) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO groups (chat_id, name, cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec, permission_mode, allowed_tools, disallowed_tools, max_subtasks, max_workers, progress_interval_sec, dispatcher_mode, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(chat_id) DO UPDATE SET\n\t\t   name                = excluded.name,\n\t\t   cwd                  = excluded.cwd,\n\t\t   default_model       = excluded.default_model,\n\t\t   max_budget          = excluded.max_budget,\n\t\t   timeout_sec         = excluded.timeout_sec,\n\t\t   permission_mode      = excluded.permission_mode,\n\t\t   allowed_tools        = excluded.allowed_tools,\n\t\t   disallowed_tools     = excluded.disallowed_tools,\n\t\t   max_subtasks         = excluded.max_subtasks,\n\t\t   max_workers          = excluded.max_workers,\n\t\t   progress_interval_sec = excluded.progress_interval_sec,\n\t\t   dispatcher_mode      = excluded.dispatcher_mode`,\n\t\tg.ChatID, g.Name, g.CWD, g.DefaultModel, g.MaxBudget, g.TimeoutSec, g.PermissionMode,\n\t\tnullableString(g.AllowedTools), nullableString(g.DisallowedTools),\n\t\tg.MaxSubtasks, g.MaxWorkers, g.ProgressIntervalSec, g.DispatcherMode,\n\t\tg.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListGroups returns all configured groups.\nfunc (d *DB) ListGroups(ctx context.Context) ([]*Group, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n\t\t FROM groups ORDER BY created_at`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar groups []*Group\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tg, err := scanGroup(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tgroups = append(groups, g)\n\t}\n\treturn groups, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// DeleteGroup removes a group and all its sessions (cascade handled manually).\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) error {\n\ttx, err := d.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback()\n\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn tx.Commit()\n}\n\ntype groupScanner interface {\n\tScan(dest ...any) error\n}\n\nfunc scanGroup(s groupScanner) (*Group, error) {\n\tvar g Group\n\tvar createdAt string\n\terr := s.Scan(&g.ChatID, &g.Name, &g.CWD, &g.DefaultModel, &g.MaxBudget, &g.TimeoutSec, &g.PermissionMode, &g.AllowedTools, &g.DisallowedTools, &g.MaxSubtasks, &g.MaxWorkers, &g.ProgressIntervalSec, &g.DispatcherMode, &createdAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tg.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\treturn &g, nil\n}\n\n// ── sessions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// GetSession returns the session for (chatID, threadID), or (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (*Session, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n\treturn scanSession(row)\n}\n\n// CreateSession inserts a new session record.\nfunc (d *DB) CreateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\tif s.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\ts.CreatedAt = now\n\t}\n\tif s.LastActive.IsZero() {\n\t\ts.LastActive = now\n\t}\n\tif s.Status == \"\" {\n\t\ts.Status = \"active\"\n\t}\n\tif s.IconColor == 0 {\n\t\ts.IconColor = 7322096 // Default to light blue (0x6FB9F0)\n\t}\n\tif s.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n\t\ts.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO sessions\n\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, model, status, icon_color, created_at, last_active, message_count, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd, summary, notification_mode, timeout_sec)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID, s.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n\t\ts.IconColor,\n\t\ts.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.MessageCount,\n\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// UpdateSession updates mutable fields on an existing session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n\t\t SET session_id    = ?,\n\t\t     cwd           = ?,\n\t\t     model         = ?,\n\t\t     status        = ?,\n\t\t     icon_color    = ?,\n\t\t     last_active   = ?,\n\t\t     message_count = ?,\n\t\t     pinned_message_id = ?,\n\t\t     total_cost_usd    = ?,\n\t\t     summary           = ?,\n\t\t     notification_mode = ?,\n\t\t     timeout_sec       = ?,\n\t\t     dispatcher_mode   = ?\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\ts.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n\t\ts.IconColor,\n\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.MessageCount,\n\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n\t\ts.DispatcherMode,\n\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionIconColor updates only the icon_color field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, color int) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET icon_color = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tcolor, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionNotificationMode updates only the notification_mode field for a session.\n// Valid modes are: \"live\" (default), \"summary\", \"quiet\".\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionNotificationMode(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, mode string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET notification_mode = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListSessions returns all sessions for a group, ordered by last_active descending.\nfunc (d *DB) ListSessions(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? ORDER BY last_active DESC`, chatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// ListAllSessions returns all sessions across all groups, ordered by last_active descending.\nfunc (d *DB) ListAllSessions(ctx context.Context) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions ORDER BY last_active DESC`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// CloseSession marks a session as closed.\nfunc (d *DB) CloseSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET status = 'closed' WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// DeleteSession removes a session record.\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionPinnedMessageID updates the pinned_message_id for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionPinnedMessageID(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, messageID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET pinned_message_id = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tmessageID, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListStaleSessions returns sessions where last_active is older than ttl.\n// Only returns sessions with status='active' — already inactive/closed sessions are excluded.\nfunc (d *DB) ListStaleSessions(ctx context.Context, ttl time.Duration) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t\t FROM sessions\n\t\t\t WHERE status = 'active'\n\t\t\t   AND datetime(last_active) < datetime('now', '-' || ? || ' seconds')\n\t\t\t ORDER BY last_active ASC`,\n\t\tint64(ttl.Seconds()),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// SetSessionStatus updates the status field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionStatus(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, status string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET status = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tstatus, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionCost adds to the total_cost_usd for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, costUSD float64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET total_cost_usd = total_cost_usd + ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tcostUSD, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ── cost_events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// RecordCostEvent inserts a new cost event record.\nfunc (d *DB) RecordCostEvent(ctx context.Context, e *CostEvent) error {\n\tif e.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\te.CreatedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO cost_events\n\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, cost_usd, input_tokens, output_tokens,\n\t\t    cache_read_tokens, cache_creation_tokens, model, from_user_id, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\te.ChatID, e.ThreadID, e.CostUSD, e.InputTokens, e.OutputTokens,\n\t\te.CacheReadTokens, e.CacheCreationTokens, nullableString(e.Model),\n\t\te.FromUserID,\n\t\te.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// GetGroupTotalCost returns the sum of all costs for a group (all threads).\nfunc (d *DB) GetGroupTotalCost(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// GetTopicTotalCost returns the sum of all costs for a specific topic/thread.\nfunc (d *DB) GetTopicTotalCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// TopicCostSummary holds cost breakdown for a single topic.\ntype TopicCostSummary struct {\n\tThreadID    int64\n\tTotalCost   float64\n\tEventCount  int\n}\n\n// GetCostsByTopic returns a list of topics with their costs for a group.\nfunc (d *DB) GetCostsByTopic(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*TopicCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT thread_id,\n\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost,\n\t\t        COUNT(*) as event_count\n\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n\t\t GROUP BY thread_id\n\t\t ORDER BY total_cost DESC`,\n\t\tchatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*TopicCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s TopicCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.ThreadID, &s.TotalCost, &s.EventCount); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// DailyCostSummary holds cost data for a single day.\ntype DailyCostSummary struct {\n\tDate    string\n\tTotalCost float64\n}\n\n// GetDailyCosts returns daily cost totals for a group within the last N days.\nfunc (d *DB) GetDailyCosts(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, days int) ([]*DailyCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT DATE(created_at) as date,\n\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost\n\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n\t\t   AND DATE(created_at) >= DATE('now', '-' || ? || ' days')\n\t\t GROUP BY DATE(created_at)\n\t\t ORDER BY date DESC`,\n\t\tchatID, days)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*DailyCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s DailyCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.Date, &s.TotalCost); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// UserCostSummary holds cost data for a single user.\ntype UserCostSummary struct {\n\tUserID     int64\n\tTotalCost  float64\n\tEventCount int\n}\n\n// GetUserTotalCost returns the sum of all costs for a user across all chats.\nfunc (d *DB) GetUserTotalCost(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE from_user_id = ?`, userID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// GetUserTopicCost returns the sum of all costs for a user in a specific topic.\nfunc (d *DB) GetUserTopicCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID, userID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND from_user_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID, userID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// GetCostsByUser returns a list of users with their costs for a group.\nfunc (d *DB) GetCostsByUser(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*UserCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT from_user_id,\n\t\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost,\n\t\t\t        COUNT(*) as event_count\n\t\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n\t\t\t GROUP BY from_user_id\n\t\t\t ORDER BY total_cost DESC`,\n\t\tchatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*UserCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s UserCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.UserID, &s.TotalCost, &s.EventCount); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// GetUserRecentCosts returns recent cost events for a user across all groups.\nfunc (d *DB) GetUserRecentCosts(ctx context.Context, userID int64, limit int) ([]*CostEvent, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, chat_id, thread_id, cost_usd, input_tokens, output_tokens,\n\t\t        cache_read_tokens, cache_creation_tokens, model, from_user_id, created_at\n\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t WHERE from_user_id = ?\n\t\t ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?`,\n\t\tuserID, limit)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar events []*CostEvent\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar e CostEvent\n\t\tvar model sql.NullString\n\t\tvar createdAt string\n\t\terr := rows.Scan(&e.ID, &e.ChatID, &e.ThreadID, &e.CostUSD,\n\t\t\t&e.InputTokens, &e.OutputTokens, &e.CacheReadTokens, &e.CacheCreationTokens,\n\t\t\t&model, &e.FromUserID, &createdAt)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\te.Model = model.String\n\t\te.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\t\tevents = append(events, &e)\n\t}\n\treturn events, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// GetSessionParticipants returns the user IDs who have participated in a session.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSessionParticipants(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) ([]int64, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT DISTINCT from_user_id FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND from_user_id > 0`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar users []int64\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar userID int64\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&userID); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tusers = append(users, userID)\n\t}\n\treturn users, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// GetSessionUserCosts returns cost breakdown by user for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSessionUserCosts(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) ([]*UserCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT from_user_id,\n\t\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost,\n\t\t\t        COUNT(*) as event_count\n\t\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND from_user_id > 0\n\t\t\t GROUP BY from_user_id\n\t\t\t ORDER BY total_cost DESC`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*UserCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s UserCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.UserID, &s.TotalCost, &s.EventCount); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionSummary updates the summary field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionSummary(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, summary string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET summary = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tnullableString(summary), chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\ntype sessionScanner interface {\n\tScan(dest ...any) error\n}\n\nfunc scanSession(s sessionScanner) (*Session, error) {\n\tvar sess Session\n\tvar createdAt, lastActive string\n\terr := s.Scan(\n\t\t&sess.ChatID, &sess.ThreadID, &sess.SessionID, &sess.CWD,\n\t\t&sess.Model, &sess.Status, &createdAt, &lastActive, &sess.MessageCount, &sess.IconColor,\n\t\t&sess.PinnedMessageID, &sess.TotalCostUSD, &sess.Summary, &sess.NotificationMode, &sess.TimeoutSec,\n\t\t&sess.DispatcherMode,\n\t)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tsess.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\tsess.LastActive, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, lastActive)\n\tif sess.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n\t\tsess.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n\t}\n\treturn &sess, nil\n}\n\n// ── allowed_users ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// IsUserAllowed returns true if userID is in the allowed_users table.\nfunc (d *DB) IsUserAllowed(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (bool, error) {\n\tvar count int\n\terr := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID,\n\t).Scan(&count)\n\treturn count > 0, err\n}\n\n// GetUserRole returns the role for a user (\"admin\" or \"user\").\n// Returns empty string if user not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetUserRole(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (string, error) {\n\tvar role string\n\terr := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT role FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID,\n\t).Scan(&role)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\treturn role, err\n}\n\n// IsUserAdmin returns true if the user has an \"admin\" role.\nfunc (d *DB) IsUserAdmin(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (bool, error) {\n\trole, err := d.GetUserRole(ctx, userID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\treturn role == \"admin\", nil\n}\n\n// GetAllowedUser returns the allowed user record, or (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (*AllowedUser, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT user_id, role, added_at FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID)\n\n\tvar u AllowedUser\n\tvar addedAt string\n\terr := row.Scan(&u.UserID, &u.Role, &addedAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tu.AddedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, addedAt)\n\treturn &u, nil\n}\n\n// UpsertAllowedUser inserts or updates an allowed user.\nfunc (d *DB) UpsertAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, u *AllowedUser) error {\n\tif u.AddedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\tu.AddedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\tif u.Role == \"\" {\n\t\tu.Role = \"user\"\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO allowed_users (user_id, role, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET role = excluded.role`,\n\t\tu.UserID, u.Role, u.AddedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// DeleteAllowedUser removes a user from the allow list.\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`DELETE FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListAllowedUsers returns all allowed users ordered by added_at.\nfunc (d *DB) ListAllowedUsers(ctx context.Context) ([]*AllowedUser, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT user_id, role, added_at FROM allowed_users ORDER BY added_at`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar users []*AllowedUser\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar u AllowedUser\n\t\tvar addedAt string\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&u.UserID, &u.Role, &addedAt); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tu.AddedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, addedAt)\n\t\tusers = append(users, &u)\n\t}\n\treturn users, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// EnsureAdminUser ensures a user exists as an admin in the database.\n// If the user doesn't exist, they are added with the admin role.\n// If they exist with a different role, their role is updated to admin.\nfunc (d *DB) EnsureAdminUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) error {\n\tu := &AllowedUser{\n\t\tUserID:  userID,\n\t\tRole:    \"admin\",\n\t\tAddedAt: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO allowed_users (user_id, role, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET role = excluded.role`,\n\t\tu.UserID, u.Role, u.AddedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ── sent_messages ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// RecordSentMessage stores a sent message so it can be found for editing later.\nfunc (d *DB) RecordSentMessage(ctx context.Context, m *SentMessage) error {\n\tif m.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\tm.CreatedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, message_id, purpose, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\tm.ChatID, m.ThreadID, m.MessageID, m.Purpose,\n\t\tm.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// GetSentMessage looks up a sent message by (chatID, threadID, messageID).\n// Returns (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSentMessage(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID, messageID int64) (*SentMessage, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, message_id, purpose, created_at\n\t\t FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND message_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID, messageID)\n\n\tvar m SentMessage\n\tvar createdAt string\n\terr := row.Scan(&m.ChatID, &m.ThreadID, &m.MessageID, &m.Purpose, &createdAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tm.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\treturn &m, nil\n}\n\n// FindSentMessageByPurpose returns the most recent sent message with the given\n// purpose in a (chatID, threadID) conversation.\nfunc (d *DB) FindSentMessageByPurpose(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, purpose string) (*SentMessage, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, message_id, purpose, created_at\n\t\t FROM sent_messages\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND purpose = ?\n\t\t ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID, purpose)\n\n\tvar m SentMessage\n\tvar createdAt string\n\terr := row.Scan(&m.ChatID, &m.ThreadID, &m.MessageID, &m.Purpose, &createdAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tm.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\treturn &m, nil\n}\n\n// DeleteSentMessage removes a specific sent message record.\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteSentMessage(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID, messageID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`DELETE FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ? AND message_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID, messageID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ── helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// nullableString returns nil for an empty string, suitable for nullable TEXT columns.\nfunc nullableString(s string) any {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn s\n}\n\n// ── subtasks ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// CreateSubtask inserts a new subtask record.\nfunc (d *DB) CreateSubtask(ctx context.Context, s *Subtask) error {\n\tif s.StartedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\ts.StartedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\tif s.Status == \"\" {\n\t\ts.Status = \"running\"\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO subtasks (id, chat_id, thread_id, parent_msg, prompt, session_id, status, result, error, started_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\ts.ID, s.ChatID, s.ThreadID, s.ParentMsgID, s.Prompt,\n\t\tnullableString(s.SessionID), s.Status,\n\t\tnullableString(s.Result), nullableString(s.Error),\n\t\ts.StartedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// UpdateSubtask updates status, result, and error for a subtask.\n// Also sets finished_at to the current time.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSubtask(ctx context.Context, id string, status, result, errorMsg string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE subtasks\n\t\t SET status = ?, result = ?, error = ?, finished_at = datetime('now')\n\t\t WHERE id = ?`,\n\t\tstatus, nullableString(result), nullableString(errorMsg), id,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// GetSubtask retrieves a subtask by ID.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSubtask(ctx context.Context, id string) (*Subtask, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, chat_id, thread_id, parent_msg, prompt, session_id,\n\t\t        status, result, error, started_at, finished_at\n\t\t FROM subtasks WHERE id = ?`, id)\n\n\treturn scanSubtask(row)\n}\n\n// ListSubtasksByStatus returns all subtasks for a topic with the given status.\nfunc (d *DB) ListSubtasksByStatus(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, status string) ([]*Subtask, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, chat_id, thread_id, parent_msg, prompt, session_id,\n\t\t        status, result, error, started_at, finished_at\n\t\t FROM subtasks\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?
